Our results bear directly on the market-effect question at the center of the fair use defense to copyright infringement.

</details>


### [116] [Notes to Self: Can LLMs Benefit from Experiential Abstractions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20372)
*Chang Liu,Xinyu Li,Artur Dubrawski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型（LLMs）能否像人类一样从经验中提取可复用的抽象知识（如策略、提醒），并利用这些抽象知识提升推理能力。实验表明，从MATH训练集的解题轨迹中提取自然语言抽象知识并构建可检索库，通过推理时检索或强化学习增强训练提示，能显著提升LLM在数学和逻辑推理基准上的表现，且自提取与教师提取效果相当，框架可跨数据集和模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类善于将经验提炼为可复用的抽象知识（如策略和警示），并逐步更有效地解决问题。本文探究LLMs是否能从类似的经验抽象中受益，即从解题轨迹中提取自然语言抽象知识，构建可检索库，用于提升推理能力。

Method: 从LLMs在MATH训练集上的解题轨迹中，由更强的教师模型或LLMs自身提取自然语言抽象知识，构建可检索库。探索两种使用模式：(1) 推理时检索（inference-time retrieval）；(2) 强化学习（RL）结合抽象增强的训练提示（abstraction-augmented training prompts）。

Result: 经验抽象显著提升了LLM在数学和逻辑推理基准上的表现。自提取的抽象与教师提取的效果相当；该抽象使用框架可迁移到其他数据集和模型。

Conclusion: LLMs能够像人类利用蒸馏经验一样，从自身经验中提取并应用可复用的抽象知识，从而提升推理能力。

Abstract: Humans distill experience into reusable abstractions, e.g., strategies and cautionary reminders, and apply them to gradually solve problems more effectively. We study whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can similarly benefit from such experiential abstractions. From LLMs' solution traces on the MATH training set, a stronger teacher or the LLMs themselves extract natural-language abstractions into a retrievable library. We explore two usage modes: (1) inference-time retrieval and (2) reinforcement learning (RL) with abstraction-augmented training prompts. Experiential abstractions improve LLM performance on mathematical and logical reasoning benchmarks. Self-extracted abstractions match teacher-extracted ones, and our abstraction usage framework can transfer to other datasets and models. These findings suggest LLMs can extract and apply experiential abstractions much as humans leverage distilled experience.

</details>


### [117] [LKValues: Aligning Large Language Models with Sri Lankan Societal Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20410)
*Nethmi Muthugala,Supryadi,Surangika Ranathunga,Nisansa de Silva,Ruijie Tao,Ovindu Gunatunga,Pengyun Zhu,Shaowei Zhang,Jingting Zheng,Deyi Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LKValues，这是首个基于调查的斯里兰卡价值观对齐资源套件，包含205人三语调查得出的40项社会价值观、15万条僧伽罗语-英语指令语料库LKvaluesIT以及1000条评估基准LKvaluesBench。实验表明现有LLM在斯里兰卡文化价值观上存在对齐缺口，而LKValues微调能有效改善Qwen系列模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的价值观对齐存在西方文化偏见，无法处理斯里兰卡等多元语言社会的本地价值观，且缺乏针对僧伽罗语的价值观基准。

Method: 通过205人的三语调查（融合全球框架与LLM引导的本地建构）提取40项多数赞同的社会价值观，构建15万条僧伽罗语-英语新闻衍生指令语料库LKvaluesIT和1000条评估基准LKvaluesBench，在多个开源和商业LLM上评估，并对三个开源基座模型进行微调。

Result: 较新较大的LLM仍存在低资源和文化价值观对齐缺口；LKValues微调改善了Qwen系列模型在英语和僧伽罗语上的表现，减少了无效输出和跨语言差异，但收益依赖模型家族。

Conclusion: LKValues能有效嵌入斯里兰卡价值观，为低资源、国家特定多元价值观对齐提供了可复现的流水线。

Abstract: Value alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) has been shown to be culturally biased toward Western norms. This results in the mishandling of local values in multilingual societies such as Sri Lanka that have their unique cultural dynamics. Existing benchmarks overlook Sri Lankan-contextualized values in its official language Sinhala, hindering culturally sensitive evaluation and fine-tuning. To bridge this gap, we propose LKValues, the first survey-grounded resource suite for Sri Lankan value alignment. From a trilingual survey of 205 respondents, blending adapted global frameworks and LLM-elicited local constructs, we derive 40 majority-endorsed societal values. Using these values, we construct LKvaluesIT, a Sinhala-English news-derived instruction corpus containing 150k scenario-based instances, and LKvaluesBench, a value-sensitive evaluation benchmark of 1,000 instances. We evaluate a set of proprietary and open-weight LLMs with LKvaluesBench. We fine-tune three open-weight base models (Qwen3.5-4B-Base, Qwen3.5-9B-Base, and Aya-Expanse-8B-Base). Our experiments show that newer and larger LLMs still exhibit low-resource and cultural value-alignment gaps. LKValues fine-tuning improves Qwen-family models in English and Sinhala, reducing invalid outputs and cross-lingual disparities, though gains remain model-family dependent. These highlight LKValues efficacy in embedding Sri Lankan values, offering a replicable pipeline for low-resource, country-specific pluralist value alignment. The dataset is publicly available at https://github.com/NextME14/LKValues.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Enterprise Integration Modernization with SAP BTP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19662)
*Shunmukha Sagar Puppala*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向SAP BTP中心的混合多云集成现代化框架（HMC-IMF），通过结合SAP Integration Suite、API管理、事件驱动架构、Clean Core扩展、安全治理和可观测性，在合成企业数据集上实现了接口复杂度降低38%、延迟从420ms降至245ms、恢复时间从155分钟降至54分钟、失败消息率从3.8%降至1.1%的显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合多云 adoption 使企业集成从狭义的中间件问题转变为战略架构问题。点对点接口和传统ESB模式导致紧耦合、重复映射、弱可观测性和安全漏洞，亟需统一的现代化框架。

Method: 提出HMC-IMF框架，以SAP BTP为中心，整合SAP Integration Suite、API管理、事件驱动集成、Clean Core扩展、运行时部署、安全治理和可观测性，构建统一现代化模型。基于包含186个应用、2850个接口、1120个API、740个事件主题、320个批处理流、5个云环境和24个月运营日志的合成数据集进行实验分析。

Result: 与遗留点对点基线相比：接口复杂度降低38%，平均集成延迟从420ms降至245ms，平均恢复时间从155分钟降至54分钟，失败消息率从3.8%降至1.1%。

Conclusion: SAP BTP在配合明确的模式选择、策略控制和运营遥测时，能够作为受治理的集成现代化层，有效应对混合多云环境下的企业集成挑战。

Abstract: Hybrid and multi cloud adoption has made enterprise integration a strategic architecture problem rather than a narrow middleware concern. Large organizations frequently operate SAP S4HANA, SAP ECC, SaaS business applications, partner portals, data lakes, and hyper scaler services across heterogeneous environments. Point-to-point interfaces and legacy enterprise service bus patterns often create tight coupling, duplicated mappings, weak observability, and security gaps. This paper proposes a Hybrid Multi Cloud Integration Modernization Framework, abbreviated as HMC-IMF, for SAP Business Technology Platform centered enterprise integration modernization. The framework combines SAP Integration Suite, API management, event driven integration, clean core extensions, runtime placement, security governance, and observability into a unified modernization model. A synthetic enterprise integration dataset is defined with 186 applications, 2,850 interfaces, 1,120 APIs, 740 event topics, 320 batch flows, five cloud environments, and 24 months of operational logs. Experimental analysis compares a legacy point-to-point baseline with the proposed BTP centered architecture. Results show a 38 percent reduction in interface complexity, a decrease in average integration latency from 420 MS to 245 MS, a reduction in mean time to recovery from 155 minutes to 54 minutes, and a decline in failed message rate from 3.8 percent to 1.1 percent. The findings suggest that SAP BTP can serve as a governed integration modernization layer when paired with explicit pattern selection, policy controls, and operational telemetry. Index Terms SAP BTP, enterprise integration, hybrid cloud, multi-cloud architecture, SAP Integration Suite, API management, event-driven architecture, clean core

</details>


### [119] [IteraSim RAG: A Multi-Stage Retrieval-Augmented Agentic Back-End for OpenFOAM-Based Computational Fluid Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20346)
*Pratyush Kumar*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出IteraSim RAG，一种基于检索增强生成(RAG)的自动化OpenFOAM算例生成后端系统。该系统通过查询扩展(物理、求解器关键词、故障排查变体)、混合检索策略(Reciprocal Rank Fusion + Maximal Marginal Relevance)、多代理分工(Architect、InputWriter、Reviewer)以及规范知识层，解决了现有RAG系统在单平面查询、单一检索策略和自审自稿三方面的局限性。在28个算例基准测试中，平均检索覆盖率达77.9%，参数修改类超90%，所有参考配置在OpenFOAM v2506上均可运行至完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenFOAM算例配置需要组装多目录输入文件(求解器、离散化、边界条件字典)，对非专业用户构成重大障碍。现有LLM+RAG系统存在三个关键局限：(1)使用单一平面查询检索；(2)对所有操作不同的请求采用同一种检索策略；(3)由同一个代理既起草又审查自己的输出。这些限制导致检索精度和生成质量不足。

Method: (1) 查询扩展：LLM将用户查询扩展为物理、求解器关键词和故障排查三种变体；(2) 融合与重排序：Reciprocal Rank Fusion(RRF)合并排名列表，Maximal Marginal Relevance(MMR)对HNSW索引的稠密向量库进行重排序；(3) 路由机制：确定性关键词路由器将工具条件化工作流查询与全局物理查询分派到不同检索路径；(4) 多代理生成：Architect(架构)、InputWriter(输入编写)和Reviewer(审查)三个代理分工协作，由静态规范知识层(求解器选择、湍流闭合、边界条件、有限体积默认值)支撑；(5) Reviewer循环：通过求解器日志和规范层诊断并修复错误。

Result: 在28个公开算例基准测试上，系统平均检索覆盖率达77.9%(中位数79.1%)，参数修改类别超90%。全部6个参考配置在OpenFOAM v2506上运行至完成。2个合成污染算例在受限Reviewer循环中仅通过求解器日志和规范层即可诊断并修复。基准测试、评分脚本及图表脚本均已公开。

Conclusion: IteraSim RAG通过查询扩展、多路径检索、多代理分工和规范知识层的组合设计，显著提升了自动化OpenFOAM算例生成的检索覆盖率和生成可靠性，有效降低了非专业用户使用开源CFD软件的门槛。

Abstract: Configuring a computational fluid dynamics (CFD) case in OpenFOAM requires assembling a multi-directory input deck of mutually consistent solver, discretisation and boundary-condition dictionaries -- a task that remains a substantial barrier to non-specialist use of open-source CFD software. Large language models (LLMs) coupled with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can lower this barrier, but existing systems retrieve with a single flat query, apply one retrieval strategy to operationally distinct requests, and let a single agent both draft and review its own output. We present IteraSim RAG, a retrieval-augmented software back-end for automated OpenFOAM case generation built around these three limitations. An LLM first expands the query into physics, solver-keyword and troubleshooting variants, Reciprocal Rank Fusion then merges the resulting ranked lists, and Maximal Marginal Relevance re-ranks the fused candidates against an HNSW-indexed dense vector store. A deterministic keyword router dispatches tool-conditioned workflow queries and corpus-wide physics queries down separate retrieval paths, and generation is split across an Architect, an InputWriter and a Reviewer agent, backed by a static canonical-knowledge layer covering solver selection, turbulence closures, boundary conditions and finite-volume defaults. On an openly released 28-case benchmark spanning zero-shot setup, few-shot generalisation, single-parameter modifications and turbulence-model swaps, the pipeline attains a mean retrieval coverage of 77.9% (median 79.1%), with the parameter-modification category exceeding 90%. All six reference configurations run to completion on OpenFOAM v2506, and two synthetically corrupted cases are diagnosed and repaired within the bounded Reviewer loop using only the solver log and the canonical layer. The benchmark, scoring rubric and figure scripts are released for reproducibility.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Identity-Truthful Online Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19964)
*Tomer Ezra,Adar Kantor*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究贝叶斯在线选择问题中的身份真实性（identity-truthfulness）限制，即决策者观察到奖励值但必须在知晓其身份（分布来源）之前决定是否接受。作者定义了身份真实性差距，证明其严格大于0.5（从而与精确为0.5的身份盲目性差距分离），并给出了0.81的上界。此外，发现存在最优身份真实性算法无法通过任何定价机制实现的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典先知不等式比较在线算法与离线最优解，近期研究关注信息差距（与知道身份到达顺序的最优在线算法相比）。本文引入身份真实性这一新限制，探索决策者在未知身份时做决策带来的性能损失，填补身份盲目性差距与顺序竞争比之间的理论空白。

Method: 定义身份真实性差距（identity-truthfulness gap）为身份真实性算法相对于最优在线基准的近似比。设计了下界算法证明该差距严格大于0.5；通过理论分析得到0.81的上界。同时构造反例揭示身份真实性与定价机制之间的不可实现性。

Result: 身份真实性差距严格大于0.5（下界），上界为0.81。该下界将身份真实性差距与精确为0.5的身份盲目性差距分离开来；上界则严格低于已知的顺序竞争比上界0.829。存在实例使最优身份真实性算法无法由任何定价机制实现。

Conclusion: 身份真实性作为一个新的信息限制模型，其性能介于身份盲目性与顺序知道之间。身份真实性与定价机制之间存在本质差异，打破了在线算法与定价之间的传统联系。

Abstract: In Bayesian online selection, a decision-maker observes a sequence of stochastic rewards and must immediately and irrevocably accept or reject each realized value. The rewards come from known distributions, referred to as their identities. Whereas classical prophet inequalities compare online algorithms to the offline optimum, recent work studies information gaps relative to the optimal online algorithm, which knows the identities' arrival order. We introduce a new restriction motivated by identity-independent decision-making: the decision-maker observes the current value but learns its identity only after deciding whether to accept it. We call such algorithms identity-truthful.
  We define the identity-truthfulness gap as the optimal worst-case approximation achievable by identity-truthful algorithms relative to the optimal online benchmark. This gap lies between the order-competitive ratio [Ezra et al. 2023] and the identity-blindness gap [Ezra et al. 2024]. We design an algorithm establishing that the identity-truthfulness gap is strictly greater than $0.5$, thereby separating it from the identity-blindness gap, which is exactly $0.5$. We complement this result with an upper bound of $0.81$ on the identity-truthfulness gap, which is strictly lower than the known upper bound of $0.829$ on the order-competitive ratio [Chen et al. 2024].
  Finally, we study the relationship between identity-truthful algorithms and pricing mechanisms. Motivated by questions on the power of posted prices in prophet inequalities [Duetting et al. 2020], we show that identity-truthfulness breaks the usual connection between online algorithms and pricing: there exists an instance for which the optimal identity-truthful algorithm cannot be implemented by any pricing mechanism.

</details>


### [121] [Improved Lower Bounds and Output Augmentation for Facility Location Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20196)
*Rafael Gomes,Sophie Klumper,Guido Schäfer,Jens Schlöter*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究在公平目标下的战略设施选址问题，证明了在欧几里得空间中，任何期望策略证明机制的近似比渐近下界为 $1 + \sqrt{d/(2(d+1))}$，并为双智能体情形提供了随机 $\sqrt{2}$-近似机制。进一步考虑输出增强框架，设计了对直线域和单位圆域的确定性及随机性策略证明机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在公平性（egalitarian）目标下，设施选址机制需要防止智能体通过谎报位置获利（策略证明性），同时尽可能接近最优的设施选址（最小化最大距离）。现有研究尚未充分揭示高维空间中策略证明机制的理论性能极限，以及输出增强（允许设施选在域外）能否带来性能提升。

Method: 推导高维欧几里得空间中期望策略证明机制的渐近近似比下界；针对双智能体情形设计随机机制；在输出增强框架下，分别针对直线域和单位圆域设计了确定性和随机性策略证明（或群体策略证明）机制。

Result: 在高维空间 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 中，任何期望策略证明机制的近似比渐近下界为 $1 + \sqrt{d/(2(d+1))}$；双智能体情形存在随机 $\sqrt{2}$-近似机制；直线域（设施可放在平面任意位置）存在确定性 $\sqrt{2}$-近似策略证明机制且下界匹配；单位圆域存在随机 $3/2$-近似群体策略证明机制。

Conclusion: 高维空间中策略证明机制的近似比存在固有下界，且该下界由大群体驱动；输出增强框架能有效提升机制性能，在某些情形下可替代随机化带来的优势。

Abstract: We study the strategic facility location problem under the egalitarian objective, where a mechanism uses the reported locations of a set of agents in Euclidean space to select a facility location that minimizes the maximum distance to any agent. We restrict our attention to strategyproof mechanisms, ensuring that no agent can benefit from misreporting their location.
  As our main results, we prove an asymptotic lower bound of $1 + \sqrt{d/(2(d+1))}$ on the approximation ratio of any mechanism that is strategyproof in expectation in $\mathbb{R}^d$. We show that this barrier is driven by large populations by providing a randomized $\sqrt{2}$-approximate mechanism for the two-agent case.
  We then consider an output-augmented framework, which allows the facility to be placed outside the agents' restricted domain. For the setting where agents are restricted to a line but the facility can be anywhere in the plane, we design a deterministic strategyproof $\sqrt{2}$-approximate mechanism with a matching lower bound, showing that output augmentation can replace the need for randomness. For the setting where the agents' reports lie on the unit circle but the facility can be placed anywhere in $\mathbb{R}^2$ we introduce a randomized $3/2$-approximate mechanism that is group-strategyproof in expectation.

</details>


### [122] [No Extra Signals Needed: The Uniform Price of Explainable Information Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20364)
*Francesco Bacchiocchi,Tommaso Cesari,Roberto Colomboni*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究一维线性设置下可解释信息设计中的解释成本（price of explainability），即使用至多K个连续区间划分状态空间的可解释信令方案与无限制方案在最坏情况下的性能比。在均匀先验下，当K=2时比值为1/2，当K≥3时比值为2/3，且这两个界都是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优信令方案常依赖随机化或将相同信号分配给状态空间中不连续的区域，导致难以解释或沟通。为解决这一局限性，作者关注可解释信息设计，即要求信令方案将状态空间划分为至多K个连续区间，并确定性发送不同信号。

Method: 对均匀先验下的线性设置进行理论分析，分别研究K=2和K≥3两种情形，推导出解释成本的确切值，并构造紧例证明最优性。

Result: 在均匀先验下，解释成本在K=2时为精确的1/2，在K≥3时为精确的2/3。这两个比值都是紧的。

Conclusion: 完全解决了Chen等人[2026]遗留的开放问题，给出了任意有界效用下可解释信令方案与无限制方案使用相同信号数量时的解释成本精确值。

Abstract: In information design, an informed sender aims to influence a receiver's decision by committing to a signaling scheme. However, optimal signaling schemes often rely on randomization or assign the same signal to disconnected regions of the state space, making them difficult to interpret or communicate. Motivated by these limitations, we focus on explainable information design in the one-dimensional linear setting, where an explainable policy partitions the state space into at most $K$ consecutive intervals and deterministically sends a distinct signal for each interval. We study the price of explainability, defined as the worst-case ratio between the optimal value achieved by an explainable signaling scheme and that achieved by an unrestricted signaling scheme using the same number of signals. Under a uniform prior, Chen et al. [2026] established a tight $2/3$ guarantee when the explainable signaling scheme was allowed to use additional signals. They also showed that the same $2/3$ guarantee holds when both the explainable and unrestricted signaling schemes use at most $K$ signals, provided that utilities are binary-valued and $K \geq 4$, leaving the case of arbitrary bounded utilities open. We resolve this question completely.
  Under a uniform prior, the price of explainability is exactly $1/2$ for $K=2$ and exactly $2/3$ for every $K \geq 3$. For both regimes, we also show that the corresponding ratios are tight.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

### [123] [MC-BRIDGE: A Modular Receiver-Chain Simulation Framework for OECT-Based Molecular Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19502)
*Hongbin Ni,Ozgur B. Akan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出MC-BRIDGE框架，用于OECT（有机电化学晶体管）基分子通信接收机的端到端建模与评估，涵盖扩散、随机结合、信号转导及噪声分析，并通过被动场测试、控制通道参考和自适应搜索优化检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OECT基分子通信接收机中，分子传输、结合、器件电流、噪声、校准和检测之间的耦合关系缺乏统一的建模和评估框架，导致系统设计和性能分析碎片化。

Method: 构建MC-BRIDGE模块化框架，连接分子释放、胞外扩散、有限面积观测、随机结合和OECT转导至电荷域检测；在独立电时间网格上生成多通道有色噪声，执行控制通道参考和电荷积分；支持MoSK、CSK及混合判决；通过共模接口估计SER和互信息，分析ISI。

Result: 在标称距离下，被动有限面积观测器仅保留中心点判决电荷幅度的27.5%；选择性通道与控制通道的相关性决定控制参考的有效性。自适应搜索后，使用与搜索和校准不相交的种子数据进行测试，给出满足SER目标的最小预算的上界。被动场测试重置受体占用、排除ISI，并在独立记录上校准阈值；保留传输和受体记忆则导致高SER。

Conclusion: 几何结构、协方差、校准和记忆等关键因素会显著改变分子通信接收机的性能结论，必须加以系统考虑。

Abstract: Organic electrochemical transistor (OECT)-based molecular communication (MC) receivers connect transport and binding to device current, noise, calibration, and detection. We present MC-BRIDGE (Molecular Communication Bioelectronic Receiver-chain Integrated Design and Guided Evaluation), a modular framework linking release, extracellular diffusion, finite-area observation, stochastic binding, and OECT transduction to charge-domain detection. On an independent electrical time grid, it generates sequence-wide multichannel colored noise, performs control-channel referencing and charge integration, and supports molecule-shift-keying (MoSK), concentration-shift-keying (CSK), and Hybrid decisions. Through common module interfaces, it estimates symbol error rate (SER) and decoded-symbol mutual information and analyzes inter-symbol interference (ISI). At nominal separation, the passive finite-area observer retains 27.5 percent of the center-point decision-charge magnitude, while correlation between the selective and control channels determines whether control referencing helps. After adaptive search, held-out records with seeds disjoint from search and calibration test the selected and next-lower budgets, yielding a tested-grid upper bound on the minimum budget meeting the SER target. This passive-field test resets receptor occupancy, excludes ISI, and calibrates thresholds on separate records at each operating point. Retaining transport and receptor memory instead produces high SER. Thus, geometry, covariance, calibration, and memory can change receiver conclusions.

</details>


### [124] [FullPASS: Geometry Optimization for Full-Duplex Pinching-Antenna Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19546)
*Morteza Barzegar Astanjin,Seyed Mohammad Azimi-Abarghouyi,Thomas Eriksson,Mikko Valkama*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出FullPASS，一种基于双平行波导的针状天线系统全双工架构。通过联合选择发射和接收针状元件，在保持自干扰低于阈值的前提下最大化双向和谱效率，并提出两阶段优化算法，在13×13和15×15网格上平均和谱效率达到穷举搜索的99.05%以内，同时将平均运行时间降低一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对全双工通信中自干扰抑制与天线选择联合优化的难题，提出一种基于针状天线的新型全双工架构及高效激活算法，以在有限候选元件集合上实现接近最优的双向通信性能。

Method: 建立包含自由空间传播、波导内传播相位/衰减及上游激活元件衰减的几何信道模型；将联合激活问题建模为二进制优化问题；提出两阶段算法：第一阶段采用相位锚定二阶锥松弛与确定性舍入生成简化模型下的二值候选激活模式，第二阶段在完整传播模型下使用交替最佳改进局部搜索（含添加、移除和交换操作）进行精细优化。

Result: 在13×13和15×15候选网格上，所提方法的平均和谱效率达到穷举搜索的99.05%以内；在15×15网格上，平均运行时间降低一个数量级以上，且可扩展至更大规模候选集。

Conclusion: FullPASS架构结合两阶段优化算法能高效实现针状天线全双工系统中的联合激活决策，在性能接近最优的同时显著降低计算复杂度，具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: This paper proposes FullPASS, an in-band full-duplex architecture for pinching-antenna systems (PASSs) based on two parallel waveguides. The FullPASS transceiver simultaneously communicates with a single full-duplex user terminal over the same time-frequency resource: the transmit waveguide delivers the downlink signal, while the receive waveguide collects the uplink signal. Candidate pinching elements are placed along both waveguides, and the FullPASS transceiver jointly selects the active transmit and receive elements. We derive a geometry-based channel model for the downlink, uplink, and transmit-to-receive self-interference paths, including free-space propagation, in-waveguide propagation phase and attenuation, and the attenuation caused by upstream activated elements along each waveguide. The joint activation problem is formulated as a binary optimization that maximizes the bidirectional sum spectral efficiency while keeping the self-interference leakage at the FullPASS receiver below a prescribed threshold. To solve the resulting nonconvex combinatorial problem, we develop a two-stage algorithm. The first stage uses phase-anchored second-order-cone relaxations and deterministic rounding to generate binary trial activation patterns under a simplified propagation model. The second stage applies alternating best-improvement local search with add, remove, and swap operations evaluated under the full propagation model. Simulations show that the proposed method achieves an average sum spectral efficiency within 0.95% of exhaustive search on both the 13-by-13 and 15-by-15 candidate grids. On the 15-by-15 grid, it reduces the average runtime by more than one order of magnitude relative to exhaustive search and remains applicable to substantially larger candidate sets.

</details>


### [125] [New lower bounds for binary constant-weight codes: $A(23,6,10)\geq 2979$ and $A(24,6,10)\geq 4214$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19550)
*Christian Lysenstoeen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文通过坐标分解和最大权独立集方法，构造了新的二元等重码，改进了A(23,6,10)≥2979、A(24,6,10)≥4214等四个下界，并证明了现有最优码的插入极大性和交换排除性质，同时分析了素数阶置换不变码的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进二元等重码A(n,d,w)的已知下界，超越Brouwer等人1990年的界限（其码表已丢失），填补组合编码理论中的空白。

Method: 采用坐标分解方法，将一半坐标固定到已知码，另一半从全交叉兼容池中利用CHILS算法选取最大权独立集；使用两类求解器进行精确计算验证插入极大性和交换排除性；对素数阶置换不变码进行循环类型分析。

Result: 构造出A(23,6,10)≥2979、A(24,6,10)≥4214、A(23,6,11)≥3539、A(24,6,8)≥1855四个改进下界，均已被Brouwer在线表格收录；证明了2969字码的插入极大性及删除至多3个码字的交换排除性；素数阶置换不变码中5+1^18类型的上界为499。

Conclusion: 坐标分解结合CHILS算法等现代计算方法能有效改进等重码下界，精确计算可验证码的极值性质，素数阶置换不变性分析为码的结构研究提供了新见解。

Abstract: Let $A(n,d,w)$ denote the maximum size of a binary constant-weight code of length $n$, minimum distance $d$, and weight $w$. We construct explicit codes proving $A(23,6,10)\ge 2979$ and $A(24,6,10)\ge 4214$. These improve the best surviving explicit codes of sizes 2969 and 4174 and surpass the corresponding 1990 bounds 2970 and 4200 of Brouwer, Shearer, Sloane and Smith, whose code listings were lost. We also obtain $A(23,6,11)\ge 3539$ and $A(24,6,8)\ge 1855$. All four bounds are now listed in Brouwer's online table. The constructions use a coordinate decomposition in which one half is fixed to a known code and the complementary half is selected from its full cross-compatible pool using CHILS for maximum-weight independent set. For the 2969-word $A(23,6,10)$ incumbent, exact computations with two solver families prove insertion maximality and exclude every improving exchange deleting at most three codewords. We also analyze codes invariant under prime-order permutations: several cycle types are excluded exactly, the $5+1^{18}$ type has upper bound 499, and reproducible heuristic saturation evidence is reported for the remaining types, with $13+1^{10}$ left open. Code files, an independent validator, model descriptions, and computational logs are released.

</details>


### [126] [Combinatorial Capacity Bounds for the $q$-ary Deletion Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19559)
*Hassan Tavakoli,Thinh Nguyen,Bella Bose*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文通过模式计数标量 \(N_n(x,y)\) 研究 \(q\) 元删除信道，导出转移概率的因子化形式、两个求和恒等式以及均匀输入下的精确闭式输出熵，进而给出有限分组容量的严格上下界、均匀输入速率的精确表达式，并通过数值实验验证了所有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为 \(q\) 元删除信道提供严格且精确的容量界分析，特别是有限分组（finite-block）场景下的容量不等式与均匀输入速率的闭式表达。

Method: 引入模式计数标量 \(N_n(x,y)\) 来分解转移概率；利用两个求和恒等式证明随机归一化；在均匀输入条件下导出输出熵的闭式解；基于这些结果构造有限分组容量夹逼界和均匀输入速率公式。

Result: 得到有限分组容量夹逼界 \((1-d)\log_2 q - h_2(d) \le C_{q,n} \le (1-d)\log_2 q\)；均匀输入速率精确公式；以及小删除概率下容量下界 \(C_q(d) \ge \log_2 q + d\log_2 d + O(d)\)。\(n=3,5,10\) 且 \(q=2,3\) 的数值实验验证了所有界。

Conclusion: 该工作为 \(q\) 元删除信道提供了有限分组容量与均匀输入速率的严格解析结果，并实验验证了其紧致性。

Abstract: We study the \(q\)-ary deletion channel via the pattern-count scalar \(N_n(x,y)\), the number of deletion subsets mapping \(x\inΣ_q^n\) to \(y\inΣ_q^k\), which factorizes the transition probability. Two sum identities on \(N_n\) certify stochastic normalization and, under uniform input, yield an exact closed-form output entropy. These give the finite-block capacity sandwich \( (1-d)\log_2 q-h_2(d)\;\le\; C_{q,n}\;\le\;(1-d)\log_2 q. \) The exact uniform-input rate is \( \frac{1}{n}I_U(X;Y) =(1-d)\log_2 q+\frac{1}{n}H_{\mathrm{Bin}}(n,1-d)-h_2(d)+\frac{Δ_n(d)}{n}, \) from which the simpler certified bound \( C_{q,n}\ge (1-d)\log_2 q-h_2(d)+\frac{Δ_n(d)}{n} \) follows. The small-\(d\) bound \(C_q(d)\ge\log_2 q+d\log_2 d+O(d)\) follows for all \(q\ge 2\). Numerical experiments at \(n=3,5,10\) and \(q=2,3\) confirm all bounds.

</details>


### [127] [Extinction Depth and q-ary Error-Correcting Codes for the Limited Permutation Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19566)
*Noam Ben Shimon,Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究半径为一的有限置换信道中的纠错编码问题，提出了"灭绝深度"这一新概念，用于判定块级联码能否在所有长度下纠正相邻交换错误。给出了显式的高码率块集，建立了检测的并行理论，并推广到任意固定位移半径的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在半径为一的有限置换信道中，错误由不相交的相邻交换构成。传统汉明距离不足以确保纠错能力，因为交换操作可能使距离很远的两个码字变得混淆。此前针对块级联码的检验方法（仅检查初始块是否碰撞）是充分但非必要的条件，存在无法认证的有效块集，因此需要更精确的判定准则。

Method: 引入"灭绝深度"概念，追踪未解决的首块对在后续块扩展中的演化过程，证明若它们在某个共同有限界处灭绝，则所有长度下均可纠错。给出了q=3和q=5的高码率显式块集。证明了不存在与块集无关的深度上界，给出了精确线性族并验证了k=3到60的二次公式，推导了多项式时间有限图检验算法。对于检测问题，建立了并行定向灭绝理论，给出了全长度块准则和精确走廊深度4k。通过稳定类型提升得到最优一阶损失√2，弱锯齿码改进了q=3,4的下界。最后通过窗口限制、取消和有界待输入前沿将准则推广到任意固定位移半径r。

Result: 给出了q=3时码率高于0.6777475和q=5时高于0.6694926的显式块集。证明了不存在块集无关的深度界。对于增长字母表，归一化纠错损失介于lnφ和ln(1.82560995)之间。显式有限长覆盖改进了有限字母表上界。检测问题中得到了精确走廊深度4k和最优一阶损失√2。

Conclusion: 灭绝深度是比传统初始块检验更精确的判定工具，能够认证更多有效的块集。该理论可统一处理纠错和检测问题，并可推广到任意位移半径的情形。

Abstract: In the radius-one limited permutation channel, errors consist of disjoint adjacent transpositions. A correcting code must separate distinct codewords: their error balls may not contain a common received word. Hamming distance does not ensure this, because disjoint swaps can make words differing in many positions confusable. For block-concatenation codes, earlier work tested each possible collision only through the longer initial block. This sufficient condition is not necessary: we exhibit a valid ternary block set that it does not certify. We introduce extinction depth, which tracks unresolved first-block pairs through later block extensions, and prove that their extinction at one common finite horizon certifies correction at every length. The criterion gives explicit $q=3$ and $q=5$ block sets with rates above $0.6777475$ and $0.6694926$. No block-set-independent depth bound exists: we give an exact linear family, verify a quadratic formula for every $3\leq k\leq 60$, and derive a polynomial-time finite-graph test. For growing alphabets, the normalized correction loss lies between $\ln\varphi$ and $\ln(1.82560995)$, while explicit finite-length covers improve finite-alphabet upper bounds. We develop a parallel directed-extinction theory for detection, including an all-length block criterion, a set unresolved by the earlier test, and exact corridor depth $4k$. Stable type lifting yields optimal first-order loss $\sqrt{2}$, and weak-zigzag codes improve the $q=3,4$ lower bounds. Finally, window restriction, cancellation, and a bounded pending-input frontier extend the criterion and its finite-state verification to every fixed displacement radius $r$.

</details>


### [128] [Bounds and Limitations on Codes Achieving List Recovery Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19576)
*Joshua Brakensiek,Yeyuan Chen,Aaron Putterman,Zihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文建立了列表可恢复码的广义Singleton界，证明了其紧性；同时指出基于AEL框架的显式构造无法突破列表恢复的已知障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 列表可恢复性是编码理论中的基本概念，广泛应用于复杂性理论、性质测试、压缩感知、流算法和密码学等领域。现有文献虽有相关结果，但缺乏紧的广义Singleton界，且显式构造方法存在根本性局限。

Method: 本文通过理论推导建立了紧的广义Singleton界，给出了速率$R^*-\varepsilon$可达而$R^*+\varepsilon$不可能的精确阈值。同时，系统化总结了Alon-Edmonds-Luby（AEL）框架，对所有基于该框架的显式构造进行了元分析。

Result: (1) 对于常数$\ell, L, \rho$和足够大的字母表$\Sigma$，当$R^*=\frac{L+1-\ell}{L}-\frac{L+1}{L}\rho$时，$(ρ,\ell,L)$列表可恢复码的速率可达到$R^*-\varepsilon$但无法达到$R^*+\varepsilon$，该界是紧的。(2) 任何基于AEL框架的显式码都无法突破加性和线性码的列表恢复障碍。

Conclusion: 本文给出了列表可恢复码的最优速率阈值，揭示了现有AEL框架在构造显式最优列表可恢复码方面的根本性局限，为未来研究提供了方向。

Abstract: In coding theory, list recoverability is a fundamental concept which robustly captures how ``spread-out'' codewords are in a code. More formally, given a code $C \subseteq Σ^n$ and input lists $S_1, \dots, S_n \subseteq Σ$ of size at most $\ell$, list recoverability requires that there are at most $L$ codewords $c \in C$ such that $c_i \in S_i$ for at least $(1-ρ)n$ choices of $i \in [n]$. List recovery is an important question which has found applications in many areas, including complexity theory, property testing, compressed sensing, streaming algorithms, and cryptography.
  As our first main result, we establish a tight ``generalized singleton bound''. Formally, we show that for constant $\ell, L,ρ$ and sufficiently large alphabets $Σ$, if we define $R^*=\frac{L+1-\ell}{L}-\frac{L+1}{L}ρ$, it is possible for a $(ρ,\ell,L)$ list-recoverable code to have rate $R^*-ε$ but impossible to have rate $R^*+ε$. One direction of our result already directly generalizes and improves a weaker impossibility result due to Goldberg, Shangguan, and Tamo.
  For our second main result, we prove that there is a fundamental shortcoming in existing methods that aim to construct explicit, optimal list-recoverable codes. Indeed, recent work has constructed explicit codes achieving list-decoding capacity (along with other related properties) using a framework introduced in the work of Alon--Edmonds--Luby (AEL). We give a meta-analysis of such constructions by presenting an ``AEL framework'' which captures all such recent constructions in the literature. Within this framework, we show that no AEL-based code can break a recently-identified list-recovery barrier for additive and linear codes.

</details>


### [129] [Learning to Transmit: Volatility-Aware Predictive Communication for Energy-Efficient IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19590)
*John Kangethe,Ifrat Ikhtear Uddin,Longwei Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于波动感知的预测通信框架 ADAPTIVEML 及其在线学习扩展 ADAPTIVEML-RLS，使 IoT 设备能根据预测残差和信号波动自适应决定是否传输数据，在三个真实数据集上实现高达 94.7% 的传输缩减，同时保持低重建误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT 网络中通信是主要能耗来源，但许多传感器测量值存在强时间相关性，传输的信息量很少。现有方法需要手动调整阈值，无法适应环境变化和非平稳性。

Method: 每个传感器维护轻量级 ML 预测器，仅当预测残差超过与局部信号波动成比例的自适应阈值时才传输数据。ADAPTIVEML-RLS 基于递推最小二乘法和指数遗忘机制，实现持续的在线适应。

Result: 在超过 240 万条传感器观测数据上，ADAPTIVEML 实现最高 94.7% 传输缩减，重建误差仅 0.352°C；ADAPTIVEML-RLS 在漂移条件下进一步降低重建误差 12-18%，同时保持 93% 以上的传输缩减。

Conclusion: 波动感知的预测通信框架能有效实现 IoT 网络的节能自适应通信，优于周期传输、静态阈值抑制、ARIMA、卡尔曼滤波等六种基线方法。

Abstract: Communication is the dominant source of energy consumption in Internet-of-Things (IoT) networks, yet many sensed measurements exhibit strong temporal correlations and provide little new information to the receiver. This paper introduces \textsc{ADAPTIVEML}, a volatility-aware predictive communication framework that enables IoT devices to intelligently decide when communication is necessary. Each sensor maintains a lightweight machine learning predictor and transmits only when the prediction residual exceeds an adaptive threshold proportional to the local signal volatility. By normalizing prediction errors using a rolling estimate of signal variability, the proposed transmission policy automatically adapts to changing environmental conditions, seasonal variations, and deployment-specific dynamics without manual threshold tuning. To address long-term non-stationarity, we further propose \textsc{ADAPTIVEML-RLS}, an online learning extension based on Recursive Least Squares (RLS) with exponential forgetting, allowing continuous adaptation to sensor drift and evolving signal characteristics. Extensive experiments are conducted on three heterogeneous real-world datasets comprising more than 2.4 million sensor observations from outdoor environmental monitoring, indoor wireless sensor networks, and urban air-quality sensing. Compared with six representative baselines, including periodic transmission, static-threshold suppression, ARIMA, Kalman filtering, EMA, and LMS filtering, \textsc{ADAPTIVEML} achieves up to 94.7\% transmission reduction while maintaining a reconstruction error of 0.352$^\circ$C. \textsc{ADAPTIVEML-RLS} further reduces reconstruction error by 12--18\% under drift conditions while preserving transmission reduction above 93\%. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of volatility-aware predictive communication for energy-efficient and adaptive IoT networks.

</details>


### [130] [CRB-Driven Beamforming and Trajectory Optimization for UAV-assisted ISAC System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19609)
*Yi Yang,Qianqian Zhang,Huaxia Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种无人机辅助的集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统，通过联合优化无人机轨迹和波束赋形，在满足通信需求的同时提升感知性能。采用深度强化学习和零空间投影方法解决非凸优化问题，仿真表明该方法相比无无人机辅助的ISAC系统将时间平均CRB降低了10%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用无人机的可控机动性和自适应感知覆盖能力，增强基站对目标的感知性能，同时保证对下行用户的可靠通信，这对未来无线网络具有实际吸引力。

Method: 联合优化无人机轨迹和波束赋形参数；采用零空间投影进行波束赋形设计；采用深度强化学习在离散时间尺度上进行轨迹优化；在每个时隙基于信道状态信息优化波束赋形以改善CRB性能并抑制干扰。

Result: 相比无无人机辅助的ISAC系统，时间平均CRB降低了超过10%；相比固定无人机轨迹和最大比传输波束赋形基准，实现了更高的感知精度。

Conclusion: 无人机辅助ISAC系统能显著提升感知性能，联合优化轨迹与波束赋形是关键，深度强化学习有效解决了非凸优化问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we study an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-assisted integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) system, where a UAV enhances the sensing capability of a base station (BS) towards a target while ensuring reliable communication towards a downlink user. This architecture is practically attractive for future wireless networks due to the UAV's controllable mobility and adaptive sensing coverage in wireless environments. The sensing performance is characterized by the average Cramér-Rao bound (CRB), which quantifies the minimum variance of the unbiased angle-of-arrival estimation. To enhance the sensing performance, the UAV trajectory and beamforming parameters are jointly optimized under power and mobility constraints, while satisfying communication requirements to the downlink user. To address the resulting non-convex problem, we employ null-space projection for beamforming design and adopt deep reinforcement learning for the trajectory optimization over a discrete-time scale. In each time slot, beamforming is optimized based on the channel state information to improve CRB performance while mitigating interference between the BS and the communication user. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly reduces the time-averaged CRB by over 10%, compared with the ISAC system without UAV assistance, and also achieves a higher sensing accuracy than both the fixed-UAV-trajectory and the maximum-ratio-transmission-based beamforming benchmarks.

</details>


### [131] [Optimal Break-Resilient Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19673)
*Canran Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对二进制码字的抗断裂码，在最多t次断裂的情况下，实现了冗余度O(t log n)，与信息论下界在常数因子内匹配，填补了此前O(t log n log log log n)与Ω(t log(n/t))之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优显式构造的冗余度为O(t log n log log log n)，而信息论下界为Ω(t log (n/t))，两者之间存在差距，本文旨在缩小这一差距。

Method: 核心思想是计算消息的短代数指纹，使解码器能够拒绝接收片段的错误组装。

Result: 当t ≤ n^{1-ε}（ε∈(0,1)固定）时，实现了冗余度O(t log n)，与信息论下界在常数因子内匹配。

Conclusion: 本文提出的抗断裂码在冗余度上达到了信息论下界的最优渐近阶，有效解决了该模型的编码问题。

Abstract: Break-resilient codes protect a word against an omniscient adversary who breaks it at arbitrary boundaries between adjacent symbols. For binary codewords of length~$n$ subject to at most~$t$ breaks, the best known explicit construction for this model has redundancy~$O(t\log n\log\log\log n)$, whereas the information-theoretic lower bound is~$Ω(t\log (n/t))$. In this paper, we close this gap by presenting a break-resilient code with redundancy~$O(t\log n)$ when~$t\leq n^{1-\varepsilon}$ for a fixed $\varepsilon\in(0,1)$, matching the information-theoretic lower bound up to a constant factor. The key idea is to compute a short algebraic fingerprint of the message, which enables the decoder to reject incorrect assemblies of the received fragments.

</details>


### [132] [Investigation of STEEP for Secure Communications Over SIMO and MISO Channels Subject to Full-Duplex Jamming and Eavesdropping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19729)
*Md Saydur Rahman,Yingbo Hua*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了STEEP方案（通过回传加密探测信号进行保密消息传输），该方案针对全双工多天线主动窃听者场景，在SIMO和MISO信道下实现了正的安全速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线通信的安全传输研究多关注SIMO和MISO信道下的保密通信，但面对具备全双工多天线且能同时进行干扰和窃听的激进窃听者(Eve)时，传统方案性能受限。本文旨在提出STEEP方案，应对这种高威胁窃听场景。

Method: STEEP方案包含两种形式：阶段1使用SIMO信道/阶段2使用MISO信道，以及反向顺序。针对Eve采用最优干扰和窃听的假设，推导两种形式下的可达安全速率表达式。在任意信道条件和有限干扰功率下，证明当阶段1功率为正且阶段2功率足够大时，安全速率严格为正。通过与常规SIMO和MISO方案在随机衰落信道和有限发射功率下进行全面比较。

Result: ①在任意信道条件和有限干扰功率下，STEEP两种形式的安全速率在阶段1功率>0且阶段2功率足够大时均为正；②建立了STEEP两种形式与常规SIMO/MISO方案之间的关联；③在随机衰落信道和有限发射功率下，对STEEP与常规方案的安全速率进行了全面对比。

Conclusion: STEEP方案能够有效抵御具备全双工多天线的激进窃听者，在传统方案性能受限的场景下实现了正的安全速率，为强窃听环境下的无线物理层安全通信提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Secure communications over SIMO and MISO channels between wireless nodes are commonly encountered in applications and widely considered in the literature. This paper investigates such a problem by considering a newly proposed scheme called secrecy-message transmission by echoing encrypted probes (STEEP). We focus on a type of heavily eavesdropped channels where an aggressive eavesdropper (Eve) has multiple antennas and is capable of jamming and eavesdropping in the full-duplex mode. Assuming optimal jamming and eavesdropping by Eve, we present the secrecy rates achievable by STEEP in two forms: using SIMO channel in phase 1 and using MISO channel in phase 2, and vice versa. With any given channel condition between every pair of nodes and any finite jamming power by Eve, the secrecy rate of STEEP in either form is shown to be positive subject to a positive power in phase 1 and a sufficiently large power in phase 2. The connections between STEEP in either form and the conventional SIMO and MISO schemes are provided, and their secrecy rates are compared comprehensively subject to random fading channels and finite transmit powers.

</details>


### [133] [Optimization of MIMO STEEP for Secure Communications over MIMOME Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19738)
*Dinglin Yu,Yingbo Hua*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种名为STEEP的安全通信传输方案，并将其应用于MIMO/MIMOME信道中。通过优化预编码器，首次揭示了MIMO STEEP可实现保密速率的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STEEP方案即使在窃听信道远强于用户信道时也能保证正保密速率，但此前尚未针对MIMO场景下的预编码优化问题进行深入研究，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对多天线用户和多天线窃听者构成的MIMO/MIMOME信道，对STEEP方案中的预编码器进行优化，以最大化可达保密速率。

Result: 通过优化预编码器，MIMO STEEP的可达保密速率相较于未优化情况有显著提升。

Conclusion: 优化预编码器能有效提升MIMO STEEP方案的保密性能，为实际安全通信系统设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: A transmission scheme for secure communications, called secret-message transmission by echoing encrypted probes (STEEP), can deliver a positive secrecy rate, even when eavesdropping channels are much stronger than that between users, subject to sufficient asymmetric power allocations from users. This paper considers an optimization problem for STEEP applied to MIMO (or MIMOME) channels between multi-antenna users and multi-antenna Eve. We reveal for the first time a significant increase of the achievable secrecy rate of MIMO STEEP using optimized precoders.

</details>


### [134] [Duality and Reverse Self-Dual Constructions for Hyperderivative Reed-Solomon Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19905)
*Hongchang Li,Zhihao Zhu,Weijun Fang,Jun Zhang,Sihuang Hu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了超导数Reed-Solomon(HRS)码的欧几里得对偶问题，通过对偶表示发现其一般不是HRS码，而是分块上三角等价于一个反向阶HRS求值码，并基于此构建了反向自对偶HRS码族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Reed-Solomon码的欧几里得对偶仍然是广义Reed-Solomon码，而HRS码作为其在Niederreiter-Rosenbloom-Tsfasman度量下的推广，其对偶结构尚不明确，本文旨在系统刻画HRS码的欧几里得对偶性质。

Method: 采用留数理论(residue-theoretic argument)推导HRS码欧几里得对偶的分量显式表示，并通过分块上三角等价变换与反向阶结构建立对偶码的刻画。

Result: 发现HRS码的欧几里得对偶一般不是HRS码，而是与反向阶HRS求值码分块上三角等价；在全域低重数情形下，三角变换退化为对角标量变换，对偶码即为HRS码的行反转；基于反向阶对偶结构，建立了反向自对偶性的显式判据，并利用加法和乘法陪集构造了若干族反向自对偶HRS码。

Conclusion: HRS码的对偶具有特殊的反向阶结构，这一发现为构造反向自对偶码提供了系统方法，丰富了对偶码理论在非Hamming度量下的认识。

Abstract: Hyperderivative Reed-Solomon (HRS) codes form a class of maximum-distance-separable codes under the Niederreiter-Rosenbloom-Tsfasman metric and may be viewed as a derivative-evaluation extension of classical Reed-Solomon codes. For generalized Reed-Solomon codes, the Euclidean dual is again a generalized Reed-Solomon code. In this paper, we investigate the corresponding duality problem for HRS codes. Using a residue-theoretic argument, we derive an explicit component-wise representation for the Euclidean dual of an HRS code. The formula shows that, in general, the Euclidean dual is not an HRS code. Instead, it is blockwise upper-triangularly equivalent to a reverse-order HRS evaluation code, where the reversal occurs in the hyperderivative orders within each evaluation block. In particular, for full-domain HRS codes with low multiplicity, the triangular transformations reduce to diagonal scalings, and the Euclidean dual is obtained as the row reversal of an HRS code. Based on this reverse-order dual structure, we further study reverse self-dual HRS codes. We establish explicit criteria for reverse self-duality and construct several families from additive and multiplicative coset structures.

</details>


### [135] [Spatial Semantic Communication: When Semantic Transmission Meets Index Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19934)
*Xinghao Guo,Yin Xu,Dazhi He,Hanjiang Hong,Zhiyong Chen,Cixiao Zhang,Yiyan Wu,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于流形天线索引调制（FA-IM）的空间语义通信系统（SSC），通过残差量化（RQ）将模拟语义特征离散化，并利用语义感知流拆分方案确保关键语义信息经历更少信道衰落，实现了RQ的高保真度、语义感知拆分的可靠性与FA-IM空间效率的有效整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字语义通信系统主要维持与传统星座调制的兼容性，而索引调制（IM）通过利用额外维度传递信息，具有更高的频谱和能量效率。本文旨在弥合IM与语义通信之间的差距，利用FA-IM技术提升语义传输性能。

Method: 1) 采用残差量化（RQ）方法将模拟语义特征离散化，以适配后续数字IM传输；2) 提出语义感知流拆分方案，将RQ与IM协同，确保关键语义信息经历更轻微的信道衰落；3) 系统兼容现有联合信源信道编码（JSCC）架构。

Result: 仿真结果验证了所提SSC系统有效整合了RQ的高保真度、语义感知拆分的可靠性以及FA-IM的空间效率，为未来数字语义传输提供了鲁棒解决方案。

Conclusion: 本文提出的SSC系统成功将残差量化、语义感知流拆分与流体天线索引调制技术相结合，在数字语义通信领域实现了性能优化，代码已开源。

Abstract: Current digital semantic communication systems have primarily focused on maintaining compatibility with conventional constellation-based modulation. In contrast, index modulation (IM) represents a more spectrally and energy-efficient alternative by exploiting additional dimensions for information conveyance. Recognizing this potential, this paper bridges the gap between IM and semantic communications by proposing a novel spatial semantic communication (SSC) system leveraging cutting-edge fluid antenna-IM (FA-IM) technology. Compatible with existing joint source-channel coding (JSCC) architectures, the proposed SSC system employs the residual quantization (RQ) approach to discretize analog semantic features for subsequent digital IM transmission. Notably, the proposed SSC system synergizes RQ and IM via a semantic-aware stream splitting scheme, which ensures that critical semantic information undergoes less severe channel fading, thereby further optimizing semantic transmission performance. Simulation results validate that the proposed SSC system effectively integrates the high fidelity of RQ, the reliability of semantic-aware splitting, and the spatial efficiency of FA-IM, thereby providing a robust solution for future digital semantic transmission. The open source code is available at: https://github.com/gxh1106/SSC.

</details>


### [136] [On Runlength Limited Codes for BICM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20026)
*Stephan Zeitz,Florian Roth,Meik Dörpinghaus,Gerhard Fettweis*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了游程受限（RLL）块码在比特交织编码调制（BICM）系统中的符号映射优化问题。针对1比特量化信道，推导了低信噪比下最优分配方案，并扩展至两状态RLL码，实验表明优化后的码字相比文献方案有显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLL码在粗量化系统中可缓解码间干扰（ISI），但信道记忆和量化非线性使得信息论分析复杂化。现有研究缺乏对RLL码符号映射方案的系统优化，需要在可分析的信道模型下建立理论框架，并指导实际系统的映射设计。

Method: 考虑1比特模数转换的块信道模型，分析BICM容量与RLL码分配方案的关系；在低信噪比下推导最优分配方案的优化问题；通过渐近大块长分析推断有限块长和块间干扰信道的实用优化策略；将方法扩展至两状态RLL（TS-RLL）码。

Result: 推导了低信噪比下RLL码最优映射方案的优化问题；给出了从渐近分析到有限块长与块间干扰信道的实用优化策略；优化后的TS-RLL码相比文献中的方案实现了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: RLL码的符号映射方案对BICM系统性能至关重要，通过信息论分析和渐近优化可有效指导实际码字设计，尤其在1比特量化及低信噪比场景下，优化映射能显著改善系统性能。

Abstract: We study the use of runlength-limited (RLL) block codes in bit-interleaved coded modulation (BICM) systems. In this setting, the RLL code acts as the symbol mapper, whose assignment between input bits and RLL symbols is critical for performance. In this work, we aim at optimizing the assignment scheme of RLL codes. One of the main applications of RLL codes is the mitigation of intersymbol interference (ISI) in systems with coarse quantization. However, channel memory and quantization nonlinearity complicate information theoretic analysis. To enable analytical treatment, we consider a block channel with 1-bit analog-to-digital conversion, modeling the transmission of a single RLL code block. For this channel, we investigate the relationship between the achievable rate in BICM systems-termed BICM capacity-and the RLL code's assignment scheme. Focusing on low signal-to-noise ratios (SNRs), we derive the optimization problem yielding the optimal assignment scheme. By looking at asymptotically large block-lengths, we infer a practical optimization strategy for RLL codes with finite block-length and channels with inter-block interference. Further, we extend this optimization to two-state RLL (TS-RLL) codes, which offer higher code rates than state independent RLL codes. We demonstrate that optimized TS-RLL codes exhibit significant performance improvements over literature counterparts.

</details>


### [137] [Learning to Decode Quantum LDPC Codes via Cluster-Based Sequential Belief Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20130)
*Mohsen Moradi,Taejoon Kim,Rémi A. Chou,David G. M. Mitchell*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于聚类的强化学习调度方法（RL-S），用于量子LDPC码的BP译码。通过将变量节点划分为固定簇，并利用排列不变的归一化直方图状态表示，实现了簇级并行更新，在保持错误率性能的同时显著减少了每轮BP迭代的调度决策次数，提供了更好的延迟-并行度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于强化学习的顺序变量节点调度（RL-S）虽然能改善BP译码性能，但其逐节点更新方式限制了迭代内的并行度，导致延迟较高。本文旨在探索如何在保持RL-S性能优势的同时提高并行性。

Method: 1) 将变量节点划分为固定簇，RL智能体每次选择一个簇，簇内所有VN并行更新。2) 引入基于局部失配权重归一化直方图的排列不变簇状态表示，结合量化使状态空间大小取决于量化分辨率而非簇大小。3) 设计相应的簇级马尔可夫决策过程、奖励函数和Q-learning更新规则。

Result: 在代表性QLDPC码上的数值结果表明，所提出的聚类学习调度保持了VN级顺序学习调度的大部分错误率性能优势，同时显著减少了每轮BP迭代的调度决策次数。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子LDPC码的BP译码提供了一种有效的延迟-并行度权衡方案，通过聚类策略在性能损失极小的前提下大幅提升并行效率。

Abstract: Belief-propagation (BP) decoding for quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes is attractive due to its low complexity, but its performance is often limited by short cycles, degeneracy, and convergence failures. Recently, reinforcement-learning-based sequential variable-node (VN) scheduling (RL-S) was shown to improve BP decoding by learning state-dependent update orders. However, the VN-by-VN nature of that approach offers limited within-iteration parallelism, since only one VN is updated at a time. In this paper, we propose a cluster-based extension of RL-S for QLDPC codes. The VNs are partitioned into fixed clusters, and at each scheduling step the RL agent selects one cluster to update, after which all VNs in that cluster are updated in parallel using the same pre-update incoming messages. To keep the tabular state space practical for large cluster sizes, we introduce a permutation-invariant cluster state based on a normalized histogram of local mismatch weights, followed by quantization. This representation makes the number of cluster states depend on the quantization resolution rather than the cluster size. We also develop the corresponding cluster-level Markov decision process, reward function, and Q-learning update. Numerical results on representative QLDPC codes show that the proposed clustered learned scheduling preserves most of the error-rate benefit of VN-level learned sequential scheduling while substantially reducing the number of scheduling decisions per BP iteration, thereby providing an attractive latency-parallelism tradeoff.

</details>


### [138] [Two-Way Wiretap Channel under Mixed Secrecy Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20172)
*Yanling Chen,Masahito Hayashi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究强单边（混合）保密约束下两跳窃听信道（TW-WC）的安全通信问题：仅要求用户1的消息对窃听者保密，用户2无保密约束。通过非自适应构造和密钥交换构造的单边简化，对任意固定自适应轮数得到指数级错误和泄漏界；利用定量一次性密码本参数传播下一轮密钥泄漏，并通过显式填充构造消除足够大块长下的初始化速率损失，推导出对应的强混合保密可达区域。非自适应构造恢复了先前弱单边单字母内界，整体自适应可达区域包含非自适应构造作为回退，并包含一个可严格更大的密钥交换子区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双向窃听信道模型中，现有工作多针对弱保密准则或双边保密约束，而强单边（混合）保密场景下缺乏系统的可达性分析；此外，自适应策略在提升可达速率区域方面的潜力尚未被充分探索。本研究旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用非自适应构造和基于密钥交换构造的单边简化方法；运用定量一次性密码本参数传播下一轮密钥泄漏；设计显式填充构造以消除初始化速率损失；对任意固定自适应轮数推导指数级错误和泄漏界。

Result: 得到了强混合保密准则下的可达区域；非自适应构造恢复了先前弱保密单字母内界；自适应可达区域包含非自适应构造作为回退，并包含一个可严格更大的密钥交换子区域。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应构造方案在强混合保密约束下优于非自适应方案，且密钥交换子区域可严格大于非自适应可达区域，表明自适应策略在双向窃听信道安全通信中具有理论优势。

Abstract: This paper studies the two-way wiretap channel (TW-WC) with an external eavesdropper under strong one-sided (mixed) secrecy: only User~1's message is required to be secure from the eavesdropper, while no secrecy constraint is imposed on User~2's message. Secrecy is measured by the information leakage of User~1's message to the eavesdropper. Applying a non-adaptive construction and a one-sided reduction of the key-exchange construction, we obtain exponential error and leakage bounds for every fixed number of adaptive rounds. A quantitative one-time-pad argument propagates the leakage of the key used in the next round, and an explicit padding construction removes the initialization-rate loss for all sufficiently large blocklengths. We derive the corresponding strong mixed-secrecy achievable regions. The non-adaptive construction recovers the previously reported weak one-sided single-letter inner bound under the stronger secrecy criterion. The overall adaptive achievable region includes the non-adaptive construction as a fallback and also contains a key-exchange subregion that can be strictly larger.

</details>


### [139] [Fundamental Limits of MIMO-OTFS and MIMO-OFDM in High-Dynamics ISAC: An Antenna Array Architecture Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20200)
*Po-Chih Chen,Ming-Chun Lee,Yu-Chih Huang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了高移动性环境下MIMO-OTFS与MIMO-OFDM集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统的性能极限，对比了稀疏阵列（SA）与均匀线性阵列（ULA）。通过随机优化框架，证明SA通过更均匀的空间特征值分布提升通信容量；在感知方面，角度CRB与阵列天线位置的二阶矩成反比，SA因更大物理孔径可实现最多为天线数量平方的精度提升。在理想条件下，两种调制方案的容量与角度估计极限相似，表明空间几何而非波形是性能提升的主要驱动力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高移动性场景（如V2X和卫星网络）面临严重多普勒频移和快速时变信道，亟需鲁棒的调制方案和高效的阵列几何结构，但目前缺乏对不同阵列配置在ISAC系统中统一的理论极限分析。

Method: 利用随机优化（stochastic majorization）框架，对MIMO-OTFS和MIMO-OFDM ISAC系统进行统一的理论分析，推导遍历信道容量和角度估计CRB的闭式表达，并对比稀疏阵列与均匀线性阵列的性能差异。

Result: 稀疏阵列在通信容量和角度估计精度上均优于均匀线性阵列：容量方面，SA通过更均匀的空间特征值分布解耦多径环境；感知方面，角度CRB与阵列二阶矩成反比，SA精度提升可达天线数平方倍。在理想条件下，MIMO-OTFS与MIMO-OFDM的容量和角度估计极限相似。

Conclusion: 空间几何（阵列结构）而非波形选择是ISAC系统在空间维度实现性能提升的主要因素，稀疏阵列在高移动性场景中具有显著优势。

Abstract: This paper investigates the fundamental limits of MIMO-OTFS and MIMO-OFDM integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) systems in high-mobility environments, specifically comparing sparse arrays (SA) against conventional uniform linear arrays (ULA). High-dynamics scenarios, such as V2X and satellite networks, suffer from severe Doppler shifts and rapidly time-varying channels, necessitating robust modulation schemes and efficient array geometries. A unified theoretical analysis of ergodic channel capacity and the Cramér$\unicode{x2013}$Rao bound (CRB) for angle estimation is provided. Utilizing the framework of stochastic majorization, the study reveals that SAs consistently outperform ULAs by creating a more $\unicode{x201C}$uniform$\unicode{x201D}$ spatial eigenvalue distribution, which decorrelates the multipath environment and increases communication capacity. For sensing, the paper proves that the angle CRB is inversely proportional to the array's second-order moment of antenna positions asymptotically, demonstrating that SAs achieve superior accuracy$\unicode{x2014}$improving by up to the square of the number of antennas$\unicode{x2014}$due to their increased physical aperture. Notably, the analysis shows that under relatively ideal conditions, MIMO-OTFS and MIMO-OFDM share similar fundamental limits for both capacity and angle estimation, suggesting that spatial geometry, rather than waveform, is the primary driver of fundamental performance gains in the spatial dimension.

</details>


### [140] [Mapped ADMM: A Robust Algorithm for 1-Bit mMIMO Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20414)
*Mohammad Amin Keshmiri,Masoud Ardakani*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于共识交替方向乘子法（CADMM）的分布式SVM方法，用于一比特大规模MIMO检测，通过分组分类器共识和星座点约束输出，显著提升了检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一比特大规模MIMO检测可被转化为二分类问题（SVM），但现有方法缺乏分布式鲁棒性。本文旨在通过分布式框架提升检测的鲁棒性和性能。

Method: 1) 将SVM重新表述为多分类器分布式形式；2) 采用共识交替方向乘子法（CADMM）实现分布式共识；3) 改进CADMM使其仅输出有效星座点，提升检测性能；4) 通过调整分组大小平衡共识准确率与分类器鲁棒性。

Result: 所提方法在性能上显著优于现有的实用一比特大规模MIMO检测方法。

Conclusion: 基于CADMM的分布式SVM方法通过共识机制和星座点约束，有效提升了一比特大规模MIMO检测的性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recently, it has been reported that one-bit massive MIMO (mMIMO) detection is equivalent to a binary classification problem that can be solved efficiently using support vector machine (SVM). Inspired by this result, we first reformulate SVM in a decentralized form consisting of multiple classifiers. This enables the use of the consensus alternating direction method of multipliers (CADMM), a technique that can improve robustness and performance through its inherent consensus making. We further update CADMM to output only valid constellation points and achieve significantly improved detection performance. In our method, by changing the size of the grouped classifiers, we balance the number of classifiers for consensus accuracy with sufficient data per group to ensure classifier robustness. Ultimately, we demonstrate that our proposed method significantly outperforms existing practically feasible methods for one-bit mMIMO detection.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [LowPowAR: Power-Constrained Tone Mapping for Augmented Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19509)
*Weikai Lin,Sheng Zhao,Ian Ross,Carl Marshall,Sushant Kondguli,Yuhao Zhu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于人类视觉感知的、学习驱动的框架，将可穿戴AR眼镜的显示功耗优化建模为功率约束下的色调映射问题，通过优化友好的色调映射算子参数化和渐进式优化策略，在给定功率预算下最大化感知质量，并将迭代优化蒸馏为轻量前馈神经网络实现实时部署。主观实验表明，在相同功率预算下，该方法比先前工作取得更好的感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常可穿戴AR眼镜面临严格的功率限制，显示器功耗是优化的关键目标，亟需一种在功率约束下最大化感知质量的显示优化方法。

Method: 将显示功耗优化建模为功率约束下的色调映射问题；提出优化友好的色调映射算子参数化及渐进式优化策略；将迭代优化过程蒸馏为轻量级前馈神经网络用于实时部署。

Result: 主观实验表明，在相同功率预算下，该方法相比先前工作取得了更好的感知质量。

Conclusion: 基于人类视觉感知的学习驱动框架能有效在功率约束下优化AR眼镜显示质量，且可实时部署。

Abstract: Everyday-wearable Augmented Reality (AR) glasses must meet strict power limits, making displays a key target for optimization. We cast display power optimization as a power-constrained tone-mapping problem and propose a human-vision-grounded, learning-based framework that maximizes perceptual quality under a given power budget. We introduce an optimization-friendly tone-mapping operator (TMO) parameterization along with a progressive optimization strategy to effectively navigate the quality-vs-power landscape. We distill the iterative optimization into a lightweight feed-forward neural network for real-time deployment. Subjective experiments show that our method yields better perceptual quality than prior work at the same power budget. Project page: https://horizon-lab.org/lowpowar/.

</details>


### [142] [A Splitting Architecture for Exact Reduced Coulomb Friction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19599)
*Hongcheng Song,Ye Fan,Uri M. Ascher,Dinesh K. Pai*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种求解精确库仑摩擦接触动力学的新方法，通过将非关联耦合显式外迭代与强凸锥约束二次规划内求解分离，在刚性体基准测试中实现了无需平滑或松弛的精确库仑互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有摩擦接触动力学方法要么修改库仑定律以提高数值鲁棒性，要么以完全耦合的单块形式求解精确律，缺乏利用问题内在结构的高效求解策略。

Method: 将精确库仑摩擦在约化形式下表述为含增广速度的锥互补问题，利用锥约束线性响应与切向速度引起的非关联耦合之间的自然分裂：外层迭代显式更新非关联耦合，内层求解强凸锥约束二次规划。

Result: 在刚性体基准测试（含粘滑转换和摩擦堆叠）上验证，无需平滑或松弛摩擦律即可复现精确库仑互补性。

Conclusion: 所提分离策略使内求解器具有模块化特性，可用不同数值方案替代而不改变外层迭代，为精确库仑摩擦的高效求解提供了新路径。

Abstract: Existing approaches to frictional contact dynamics typically either modify the Coulomb law to improve numerical robustness or solve the exact law in a fully coupled monolithic form. However, in its reduced form, exact Coulomb friction can be written as a cone complementarity problem with an augmented velocity, which reveals a natural split between a cone-constrained linear response and a scalar non-associated coupling induced by tangential velocity. We exploit this structure in the solver design. Our method uses an outer iteration to update the non-associated coupling explicitly, and an inner solve for a strongly convex cone-constrained quadratic program. This separation also makes the inner solver modular, so different numerical schemes can be used without changing the outer iteration. We evaluate the method on rigid-body benchmarks with stick-slip transitions and frictional stacking, and show that it reproduces exact Coulomb complementarity without smoothing or relaxing the friction law.

</details>


### [143] [Fast Wave-optics Rendering of Multiplane Images for 3D Holographic Displays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19731)
*Brian Chao,Dario Seyb,Nathan Matsuda,Oliver Cossairt,Yang Zhou,Douglas Lanman,Gordon Wetzstein,Grace Kuo,Changwon Jang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多平面图像（MPI）的波动光学渲染管线，用于高效高质量的全息图合成，相比最先进的基于图元的CGH算法实现了高达250,000倍的加速，同时保持相当图像质量，并显著优于传统基于层的CGH算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经渲染技术虽然能实现从稀疏图像重建3D场景和新视角合成，但最终渲染结果仍以2D图像呈现在传统屏幕或VR头显上，限制了感知真实感和沉浸感。随着新型3D场景表示的发展，亟需专用渲染算法将这些3D内容转化为兼容全息显示等新兴3D显示技术的格式。

Method: 提出了一种基于多平面图像（MPI）的波动光学渲染管线，用于计算机生成全息图（CGH）的高效合成。利用MPI作为中间表示，结合波动光学原理进行全息图计算。

Result: 在运行时间上，相比最先进的基于图元的CGH算法实现了高达250,000倍的加速，同时保持相当的图像质量；在图像质量上显著优于传统基于层的CGH算法。在多种3D场景数据集上，通过仿真和实验验证了优异的3D焦堆栈和4D光场重建性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的MPI-based CGH算法在效率和图像质量之间取得了优异的平衡，为将神经渲染生成的3D内容高效转化为全息显示兼容格式提供了可行方案，推动了沉浸式3D显示技术的发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in neural rendering have unlocked unprecedented capabilities in 3D reconstruction and novel view synthesis, giving rise to applications such as virtual fly-throughs of a 3D scene reconstructed from a set of sparse, casually captured images. However, these renderings are viewed on a computer screen or conventional VR headsets as 2D images, greatly limiting the perceptual realism and immersiveness of such experiences. The rapid development in novel 3D scene representations calls for dedicated rendering algorithms that convert these readily-available 3D contents into formats that are compatible with emerging 3D display technologies, such as holographic displays. In this paper, we propose a wave-optics rendering pipeline that works with multiplane images (MPIs) for efficient and high-quality hologram synthesis. Our MPI-based computer-generated holography algorithm greatly outperforms state-of-the-art primitive-based CGH algorithms in terms of runtime, achieving speedups up to 250,000x while achieving comparable image quality, and significantly outperforms conventional layer-based CGH algorithms in terms of image quality. We validate our method extensively on a wide variety of 3D scene datasets both in simulation and through experimentally captured results, showing exceptional 3D focal stack and 4D light field reconstruction performance without sacrificing efficiency.

</details>


### [144] [MR-Compare: A Mixed-Reality Framework for Spatially Grounded Visual Comparison of 3D Gaussian Splatting and Mesh Reconstructions with the Physical Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20325)
*Changrui Zhu,Ernst Kruijff,Pengju Zhang,Simon Julier*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出MR-Compare，一个混合现实框架，用于3D高斯泼溅与网格重建的空间化视觉对比，支持实时视频透视。在Meta Quest 3上实现，采用两阶段配准流程和3D滑块进行跨媒体比较。通过真实世界基准测试和30人用户研究，验证了厘米级平移误差、高可用性和低工作量。还提出各向异性滤波器改进3DGS配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D重建质量评估缺乏直观的空间可视化对比工具，特别是不同重建表示（3D高斯泼溅 vs 网格）在混合现实环境中的直接比较尚未被研究。

Method: 提出了MR-Compare框架，包含：(1) 两阶段配准流程实现空间对齐；(2) 3D滑块实现跨媒体（3DGS vs 网格）对比；(3) 各向异性滤波器作为零样本模块，利用高斯各向异性改进3DGS配准。在PC连接的Meta Quest 3上实现，通过五种桌面和移动重建工作流及30人用户研究进行验证。

Result: 所有工作流均实现厘米级平移误差；3DGS-MCMC配准误差最低且VST视觉一致性最强；房间会话测量显示高感知可用性和低工作量；各向异性滤波器的Replica阈值扫描表明适度剪枝可提高鲁棒性并降低残差。

Conclusion: MR-Compare在测试的静态室内场景中建立了系统级可行性，验证了混合现实中3DGS与网格比较的有效性，但尚未达到任务级有效性或独立部署水平。

Abstract: We introduce MR-Compare, a mixed reality framework for spatially grounded visual comparison between 3D Gaussian splatting and mesh reconstructions with live video see-through (VST). Implemented on a PC-tethered Meta Quest~3, it combines a two-stage registration pipeline with a 3D Slider for cross-media comparison. We evaluated five representative desktop and mobile reconstruction workflows through a real-world benchmark with an exploratory user study ($n=30$) in two static indoor rooms. MR-Compare achieved centimetre-level translation error across all workflows. The two desktop 3DGS workflows showed the strongest overall pattern, with 3DGS-MCMC yielding the lowest registration error and strongest VST-referenced visual consistency. Room-session measures indicated high perceived usability and low workload. We further propose an anisotropy filter, a zero-shot module that leverages Gaussian anisotropies to improve 3DGS registration in MR-Compare. A controlled Replica threshold sweep shows that moderate pruning can improve robustness and reduce residual errors. These results establish system-level feasibility in the tested setting rather than task-level effectiveness or standalone deployment. The project is available at https://github.com/changruizhu96/MR-Compare.

</details>


### [145] [Split Radiance Cascades: Real-Time Global Illumination via Sparse Radiance Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20384)
*Rouli Freeman,Alexander Sannikov*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出Split Radiance Cascades，通过稀疏哈希图存储世界空间探针并引入光线分裂技术，实现了高质量实时3D漫射全局光照，在单帧和时域累积场景中均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Radiance Cascade方法在2D或屏幕空间运行，存储高细节体积辐射信息的成本过高，导致无法高效应用于3D实时漫射全局光照。

Method: 使用稀疏哈希图存储世界空间探针以降低存储开销；引入光线分裂（ray splitting）技术，从可见表面追踪光线并根据命中距离计算辐射区间对级联的贡献。

Result: 在多种场景下验证了算法效果，表明Split Radiance Cascades在单帧和时域累积两种上下文中均能提供高质量间接光照。

Conclusion: 该方法在保证实时性能的同时，有效解决了Radiance Cascade在3D应用中的存储瓶颈，实现了细节丰富且无噪声/锯齿的漫射全局光照。

Abstract: Radiance probe methods are a popular and well-tested approach for approximating diffuse global illumination for real-time graphics, but they commonly suffer from a lack of detail due to the large spacing between probes. Radiance Cascade (RC) fixes this by increasing spatial resolution and reducing angular resolution for light and occlusion from closer objects, which allows it to provide details at all scales without noise or aliasing. However, leading implementations of RC either run in 2D or screenspace, due to the prohibitive costs of storing high-detail volumetric radiance information.
  In this work, we adapt Radiance Cascades for accurate real-time 3D diffuse global illumination using a sparse hashmap to store world-space probes. We introduce ray splitting, a method for calculating radiance intervals used in RC by tracing rays from visible surfaces and calculating their contribution to cascades based on their hit distance. We evaluate our algorithm, Split Radiance Cascades, on a variety of scenes, and demonstrate that it can provide high-quality indirect illumination in both single-frame and temporally accumulated contexts.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [FineServe: A Fine-Grained Dataset and Characterization of Global LLM Serving Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19349)
*Tiancheng Zhang,Shaoyuan Huang,Mingyuan Wang,Yunfeng Zhao,Xiaofei Wang,Wenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FineServe是一个从全球商业平台收集的多模型LLM服务负载数据集，揭示了不同模型架构、规模和任务意图下的到达动态和令牌行为波动规律，并提供了细粒度的工作负载生成器，为多模型服务平台的评估提供现实基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究依赖代理痕迹或粗粒度特征，无法捕捉现代多模型LLM平台的异构性，缺乏对真实世界服务负载的深入理解。

Method: 从全球商业市场收集在野多模型LLM服务负载数据集FineServe，进行到达动态和令牌行为的细粒度分析，并构建可组合的模型感知工作负载生成器。

Result: 揭示了不同模型架构、规模和任务意图下存在根本不同的波动模式；开发了FineServe工作负载生成器，可将细粒度模型感知负载组合成可配置的混合负载。

Conclusion: FineServe为评估LLM服务系统中的路由、调度和容量规划策略提供了现实基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as always-on online services, making efficient LLM serving a critical systems challenge. Achieving low latency and high throughput under volatile demand requires deep understanding of real-world serving workloads, yet existing studies often rely on proxy traces or coarse-grained characterizations that fail to capture the heterogeneity of modern multi-model LLM platforms. We present FineServe, an in-the-wild, multi-model LLM serving workload dataset collected from a global commercial marketplace, enabling fine-grained characterization of real-world serving dynamics across heterogeneous models and tasks. Leveraging FineServe, we conduct a comprehensive analysis of arrival dynamics and token behavior, revealing fundamentally different fluctuation regimes across model architectures, scales and task intents. Building on these insights, we develop the FineServe workload generator, which composes fine-grained model-aware workloads into configurable mixtures tailored for benchmarking multi-model serving platforms. By exposing these fine-grained workload dynamics, FineServe provides a realistic foundation for evaluating routing, scheduling, and capacity-planning strategies in LLM serving systems. FineServe is available at https://github.com/hihiztc1/FineServe.

</details>


### [147] [Hybrid LSTM-Graph Neural Framework for Robust Financial Fraud Detection and Adversarial Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19350)
*Mariam Zakaria Moussa Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 FraudShield AI，一种融合 LSTM 与图拓扑特征的混合框架，用于检测金融反洗钱中的复杂欺诈模式（如结构化交易和分层交易），在 PaySim 数据集上显著优于 Logistic Regression 和 XGBoost 基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机构在检测复杂洗钱模式（如 smurfing 和 layering）时面临极端数据不平衡（欺诈率仅 0.13%）以及对手不断演化的规避手段等严峻挑战，现有方法难以有效应对。

Method: 将长短期记忆网络与手工设计的图拓扑特征（PageRank 中心性、入度动态、自定义 Flow Ratio）相结合，使用 Focal Loss 处理类别不平衡，并引入动态阈值机制以增强对低值 smurfing 攻击的鲁棒性。

Result: 在 PaySim 数据集上，该混合模型在精确率、召回率和 F1 分数上显著优于 Logistic Regression 和 XGBoost，尤其在难检测的微交易欺诈模式上表现突出。消融实验验证了时序组件与拓扑组件之间的互补贡献。

Conclusion: 将时序建模与图拓扑特征相结合的混合方法能够更有效地从网络级取证角度检测洗钱欺诈，为金融反欺诈提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Financial institutions face significant challenges in detecting sophisticated money laundering patterns, such as smurfing and layering, due to extreme data imbalance (0.13% fraud rate) and evolving adversarial evasion tactics. This paper proposes FraudShield AI, a hybrid framework that integrates Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks with hand-crafted Graph Topological Features to capture both temporal sequences and structural relational context. By engineering network-centric features including PageRank Centrality, In-Degree dynamics, and a custom Flow Ratio, the system shifts the detection paradigm from isolated transaction analysis to network-level forensics. A Focal Loss objective is used to address class imbalance, and a dynamic thresholding mechanism is introduced to improve resilience against low-value smurfing attacks. Experimental evaluation on the PaySim dataset shows that the proposed hybrid model substantially outperforms Logistic Regression and XGBoost baselines in Precision, Recall, and F1-Score, particularly on hard-to-detect micro-transaction fraud patterns. An ablation study confirms the complementary contribution of both the temporal and topological components.

</details>


### [148] [OpenEvoShield: Dual Non-Stationary Continual Defense for Open-World Multi-Agent System Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19351)
*Litian Zhang,Chaozhuo Li,Yuting Zhang,Zejian Chen,Bingyu Yan,Qiwei Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OpenEvoShield，一种针对LLM多智能体系统中恶意指令注入攻击的共演化持续防御框架，通过非对称速率控制、动态行为边界、EWC正则化策略集成和多粒度能量检测器，在动态攻防对抗中持续保持高效检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统在安全关键场景中面临双重动态威胁：攻击者不断调整注入策略以绕过防御，同时正常智能体行为随系统扩展发生漂移。现有防御方法将部署视为封闭世界问题，一旦分布偏移超出训练覆盖范围，性能迅速下降。

Method: 提出OpenEvoShield框架，包含四个模块：M1非对称速率控制器（解耦攻击侧快学习率与正常侧慢学习率）、M2正常边界更新器（以慢速率维护动态行为边界）、M3 EWC正则化策略集成（快速适应无灾难性遗忘）、M4基于能量的多粒度检测器（融合节点、子图、图级别证据检测OOD攻击）。

Result: 在5个基准、4种MAS拓扑结构、100轮部署实验中，OpenEvoShield优于静态和持续基线方法，能检测大部分先前未见过的攻击，同时保持较低的假阳性率。

Conclusion: OpenEvoShield通过共演化持续防御框架有效应对LLM多智能体系统中的双重动态威胁，为动态攻防场景下的安全部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications, where adversaries inject malicious instructions through inter-agent communication to propagate harmful behaviors. Unlike static threats, these attacks are doubly dynamic: adversaries refine injection strategies against deployed defenses while normal-agent behavior drifts with system expansion. Existing defenses treat deployment as a closed-world problem and degrade rapidly once either distribution shifts beyond training coverage. We propose OpenEvoShield, a co-evolutionary continual defense framework for LLM-MAS. An asymmetric rate controller (M1) decouples fast attack-side and slow normal-side learning rates from dual drift signals. A normal-boundary updater (M2) maintains a dynamic behavioral boundary at the slow rate, while an EWC-regularized policy ensemble (M3) fast-adapts without catastrophic forgetting. An energy-based multi-granularity detector (M4) fuses node-, subgraph-, and graph-level evidence to classify novel attacks as out-of-distribution. Experiments over 100 deployment rounds across five benchmarks and four MAS topologies show that OpenEvoShield outperforms static and continual baselines, detecting most previously unseen attacks while keeping false positive rates low.

</details>


### [149] [Benchmarking Confidential GPU Inference on NVIDIA H100 under Intel TDX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19353)
*Wei Wang,Abdul Hyee Waqas,Burns Smith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在单张NVIDIA H100 GPU（Intel TDX机密实例）上，对比了Mistral-7B和Qwen3-30B-A3B两个大语言模型在标准执行与机密计算模式下的推理性能。机密模式下，首Token延迟增加21.8%-27.8%，全局Token吞吐量下降17.7%-21.1%，且大模型更早达到饱和拐点。结论是机密推理在负载下仍可保持可用吞吐量，但容量规划需考虑吞吐量惩罚及大模型的早期饱和行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密计算正成为处理敏感输入或保护模型资产的AI推理工作负载的实际部署需求，但启用GPU加速的大语言模型服务的机密执行所带来的性能代价仍与工作负载相关且具有操作重要性，亟需量化评估。

Method: 在单张NVIDIA H100 80GB GPU（Intel TDX机密实例）上，以Mistral-7B v0.1和Qwen3-30B-A3B为代表性模型，对比标准非机密执行与机密计算模式。测量指标包括：首Token时间、端到端请求延迟、单请求Token生成吞吐量、全局Token吞吐量及闭环请求吞吐量，并在递增并发度下进行固定请求率实验和闭环并发实验。

Result: 固定请求率实验中，机密模式使Mistral-7B平均TTFT增加21.8%，Qwen3-30B-A3B增加27.8%；全局Token吞吐量分别下降17.7%和21.1%。闭环并发实验中，吞吐量差距为11.5%-20.2%，但Qwen3-30B-A3B在机密模式下更早达到饱和拐点。

Conclusion: 机密GPU推理在负载下仍可保持可用吞吐量，但容量规划必须同时考虑稳态吞吐量惩罚以及大模型在机密模式下表现出的更早饱和行为。

Abstract: Confidential computing is becoming a practical deployment requirement for AI inference workloads that process sensitive inputs or protect proprietary model assets. However, the performance cost of enabling confidential execution for GPU-accelerated large language model serving remains workload dependent and operationally important. This paper presents a benchmark study comparing standard non-confidential execution with confidential computing mode on a single NVIDIA H100 80GB GPU hosted in an Intel TDX confidential instance. The evaluation uses two representative language models, Mistral-7B v0.1 and Qwen3-30B-A3B, and measures time to first token, end-to-end request latency, per-request token generation throughput, global token throughput, and closed-loop request throughput under increasing concurrency. In fixed request-rate experiments, confidential mode increases average TTFT by 21.8% for Mistral-7B and 27.8% for Qwen3-30B-A3B, while global token throughput drops by 17.7% and 21.1%, respectively. In closed-loop concurrency experiments, throughput gaps remain in the 11.5-20.2% range, but the larger model reaches its saturation knee earlier under confidential mode. The results suggest that confidential GPU inference can retain usable throughput under load, but capacity planning must account for both the steady throughput penalty and the earlier saturation behavior observed for larger models.

</details>


### [150] [FormulaSPIN: Self-Play Fine-Tuning for Natural Language to Spreadsheet Formula Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19354)
*Cy Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FORMULASPIN是一个自对弈框架，利用公式的可执行性作为隐式监督信号，通过两阶段对抗训练和ExecVote投票机制，在无需额外数据的情况下突破了监督微调的性能上限，在电子表格公式生成任务上达到SOTA（74.9%精确匹配，87.1%执行准确率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子表格公式写作是数百万用户面临的主要障碍，现有方法依赖静态监督数据，在有限标注上迅速饱和。传统SFT方法存在性能天花板，而直接应用自对弈框架（SPIN）会因执行等价变体被矛盾地惩罚而产生梯度冲突。

Method: 提出FORMULASPIN框架，核心创新包括：(1) 利用公式的二元可执行性作为隐式监督，区分语义错误与有效风格变体；(2) 设计两玩家博弈训练范式，主玩家学习偏好当前真实公式而非旧版本公式，同时执行反馈将输出划分为不同粒度，实现从语义正确性到风格优化的自适应课程学习；(3) 引入ExecVote机制，通过执行结果投票自然地处理多个有效公式变体。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上达到SOTA：NL2FORMULA任务上精确匹配74.9%、执行准确率87.1%，性能与使用额外偏好标注训练的模型相当，且超越了传统SFT方法和前沿闭源模型。

Conclusion: FORMULASPIN证明了自对弈在数据稀缺任务中的潜力，为将自对弈扩展到可执行领域之外开辟了道路。

Abstract: Spreadsheet applications are used by hundreds of millions worldwide, yet writing formulas remains a significant barrier. Existing approaches rely on static supervised data, which quickly saturates on limited annotations. In this paper, we introduce FORMULASPIN, a self-play framework that breaks the ceiling of supervised fine-tuning by enabling iterative self-improvement without any additional data. Vanilla SPIN fails on this task: it uniformly penalizes every non-matching output, so execution-equivalent alternatives are punished as negatives in one example while serving as ground truth in another, producing contradictory gradients. Our framework resolves this by exploiting formula generation's unique advantage: binary executability provides implicit supervision that separates semantic errors from valid stylistic variants. We frame training as a two-player game in which the main player learns to prefer ground-truth formulas over those from its previous version, while execution feedback sorts outputs into distinct granularities-enabling an adaptive curriculum that shifts from semantic correctness to stylistic refinement. To further increase accuracy, we incorporate ExecVote, a semantic-level voting mechanism that naturally handles multiple valid formulations. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that FORMULASPIN achieves state-of-the-art performance, with 74.9% exact match and 87.1% execution accuracy on NL2FORMULA, matching models trained with additional preference annotations while outperforming both traditional SFT and frontier proprietary models. These findings underscore self-play's potential to tackle scarce data tasks and open the door to extending it beyond executable domains.

</details>


### [151] [Information Discernment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19355)
*Joshua Ashkinaze,Laura Kurek,Alina Faisal,Tongyuan Miao,Mariam Joseph,Ceren Budak,Eric Gilbert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出信息辨别力（Information Discernment）概念，并构建Learn2Discern (L2D)基准框架。通过三个规范性公理和用户研究（n=299）验证其外部有效性。实验覆盖13个模型、近67万次试验，发现模型在来源辨别和真值辨别上均接近随机水平，更依赖来源流行度而非可靠性，且新模型和大模型仅改善真值辨别而未改善来源辨别。提出简单推理时干预可提升两种辨别力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地使用外部知识源（如互联网），但模型是否能够恰当权衡信息权重——对可靠来源更新更多（来源辨别）以及对使先验更接近真相的声称更新更多（真值辨别）——尚不明确。现有研究缺乏系统框架来评估这一核心对齐属性。

Method: 形式化定义信息辨别力，提出基于三个规范性公理的Learn2Discern (L2D)实验框架与基准。通过预注册、配额匹配的用户研究（n=299）验证公理的外部有效性。在13个模型、近67万次试验上进行大规模实验评估。

Result: 模型在来源辨别和真值辨别上均接近随机水平；对来源流行度的依赖程度是对来源可靠性的两倍；无论声称是否改善或恶化相对于真实情况的位置，模型更新的程度大致相同；模型在自身先验已最准确的数据集上整合外部知识最有效；新模型和大模型改善了真值辨别但未改善来源辨别。

Conclusion: 模型存在系统性的信息辨别失败，来源辨别是模型复杂化无法解决的盲点。简单推理时干预可改善两种辨别力。随着LLM取代传统搜索引擎，这一对齐属性重要性不断提升。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used with external knowledge sources like the internet. Do they weigh information appropriately -- updating more for reliable sources (source discernment) and more when claims bring priors closer to the truth (truth discernment)? We formalize this as information discernment and introduce Learn2Discern (L2D), an experimental framework and benchmark grounded in three normative axioms with interpretable metrics. To establish external validity, a pre-registered, quota-matched user study (n=299) confirms that real LLM users endorse all three axioms and report that violations reduce their trust and usage intent. Across 13 models and nearly 670K trials, we find consistent failures across both dimensions: models perform near chance on source and truth discernment, rely on source popularity twice as much as source reliability, and update roughly equally whether a claim improves or worsens their position relative to the ground truth. Models integrate external knowledge most effectively on datasets where their priors are already the most accurate. Newer and larger models improve truth discernment but not source discernment, a blind spot that model complexity does not address. We identify simple inference-time interventions that improve both forms of discernment. We release our dataset and survey as a testbed for a core alignment property that scales in importance as LLMs replace traditional search.

</details>


### [152] [NEXUS: Structured Runtime Safety for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19356)
*Elias Hossain,Md Mehedi Hasan Nipu,Tasfia Nuzhat Ornee,Rajib Rana,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NEXUS是一个用于LLM智能体运行时安全监控的结构化计划监控器，通过结合确定性规则、参数级检查和校准的逻辑回归风险评分，实现允许、阻止、请求确认或请求修改四种干预动作，在多个基准测试上优于纯规则方法，且延迟极低（0.205毫秒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着使用工具的LLM智能体越来越多地执行高风险操作，运行时安全监控变得至关重要，现有方法缺乏细粒度的结构化干预策略。

Method: NEXUS结合了确定性安全规则、参数级检查以及经过校准的逻辑回归风险评分，实现四种干预动作（允许、阻止、请求确认、请求修改）的分级升级策略。

Result: 在128实例的合成基准上F1为0.949，4类干预准确率0.6406，比纯规则方法高27.3个百分点；R-Judge上F1=0.861 vs 0.849；IPI上0% ASR且99%控制允许；NEXUS-Stress上F1=0.881；中位延迟仅0.205毫秒。

Conclusion: NEXUS在保持极低延迟开销（低于0.1%）的同时，显著提升了LLM智能体运行时安全监控的细粒度干预能力，代码和基准已公开。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents increasingly execute high-impact actions, making runtime safety monitoring essential. We present NEXUS (Neural EXecution Utility and Safety), a structured-plan safety monitor that applies a formal intervention policy to select among four actions: allow, block, request confirmation, or request revision. NEXUS combines deterministic safety rules, argument-level inspection, and a calibrated logistic-regression risk score for graded escalation.
